<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💡 What is RAGFlow?</h2><a id="user-content--what-is-ragflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💡 What is RAGFlow?" href="#-what-is-ragflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://ragflow.io/" rel="nofollow">RAGFlow</a> is a leading open-source Retrieval-Augmented Generation (<a href="https://ragflow.io/basics/what-is-rag" rel="nofollow">RAG</a>) engine that fuses cutting-edge RAG with Agent capabilities to create a superior context layer for LLMs. It offers a streamlined RAG workflow adaptable to enterprises of any scale. Powered by a converged <a href="https://ragflow.io/basics/what-is-agent-context-engine" rel="nofollow">context engine</a> and pre-built agent templates, RAGFlow enables developers to transform complex data into high-fidelity, production-ready AI systems with exceptional efficiency and precision.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🎮 Get Started</h2><a id="user-content--get-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🎮 Get Started" href="#-get-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Try our cloud service at <a href="https://cloud.ragflow.io" rel="nofollow">https://cloud.ragflow.io</a>.</p>
<div align="center" dir="auto">
<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/infiniflow/ragflow-docs/refs/heads/image/image/chunking.gif"><img alt="Chunking demonstration" src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/infiniflow/ragflow-docs/refs/heads/image/image/chunking.gif" width="1200" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/infiniflow/ragflow-docs/refs/heads/image/image/agentic-dark.gif"><img alt="Agentic workflow demonstration" src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/infiniflow/ragflow-docs/refs/heads/image/image/agentic-dark.gif" width="1200" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🌟 Key Features</h2><a id="user-content--key-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🌟 Key Features" href="#-key-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🍭 <strong>"Quality in, quality out"</strong></h3><a id="user-content--quality-in-quality-out" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🍭 &quot;Quality in, quality out&quot;" href="#-quality-in-quality-out"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/deepdoc/README.md">Deep document understanding</a>-based knowledge extraction from unstructured data with complicated
formats.</li>
<li>Finds "needle in a data haystack" of literally unlimited tokens.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🍱 <strong>Template-based chunking</strong></h3><a id="user-content--template-based-chunking" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🍱 Template-based chunking" href="#-template-based-chunking"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Intelligent and explainable.</li>
<li>Plenty of template options to choose from.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🌱 <strong>Grounded citations with reduced hallucinations</strong></h3><a id="user-content--grounded-citations-with-reduced-hallucinations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🌱 Grounded citations with reduced hallucinations" href="#-grounded-citations-with-reduced-hallucinations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Visualization of text chunking to allow human intervention.</li>
<li>Quick view of the key references and traceable citations to support grounded answers.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🍔 <strong>Compatibility with heterogeneous data sources</strong></h3><a id="user-content--compatibility-with-heterogeneous-data-sources" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🍔 Compatibility with heterogeneous data sources" href="#-compatibility-with-heterogeneous-data-sources"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Supports Word, Slides, Excel, TXT, images, scanned copies, structured data, web pages, and more.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🛀 <strong>Automated and effortless RAG workflow</strong></h3><a id="user-content--automated-and-effortless-rag-workflow" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🛀 Automated and effortless RAG workflow" href="#-automated-and-effortless-rag-workflow"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Streamlined RAG orchestration catered to both personal and large businesses.</li>
<li>Configurable LLMs as well as embedding models.</li>
<li>Multiple recall paired with fused re-ranking.</li>
<li>Intuitive APIs for seamless integration with business.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔎 System Architecture</h2><a id="user-content--system-architecture" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔎 System Architecture" href="#-system-architecture"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🎬 Self-Hosting</h2><a id="user-content--self-hosting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🎬 Self-Hosting" href="#-self-hosting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📝 Prerequisites</h3><a id="user-content--prerequisites" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📝 Prerequisites" href="#-prerequisites"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>CPU &gt;= 4 cores</li>
<li>RAM &gt;= 16 GB</li>
<li>Disk &gt;= 50 GB</li>
<li>Docker &gt;= 24.0.0 &amp; Docker Compose &gt;= v2.26.1</li>
<li>Python &gt;= 3.13</li>
<li><a href="https://gvisor.dev/docs/user_guide/install/" rel="nofollow">gVisor</a>: Required only if you intend to use the code executor (sandbox) feature of RAGFlow.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-tip" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-light-bulb mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 1.5c-2.363 0-4 1.69-4 3.75 0 .984.424 1.625.984 2.304l.214.253c.223.264.47.556.673.848.284.411.537.896.621 1.49a.75.75 0 0 1-1.484.211c-.04-.282-.163-.547-.37-.847a8.456 8.456 0 0 0-.542-.68c-.084-.1-.173-.205-.268-.32C3.201 7.75 2.5 6.766 2.5 5.25 2.5 2.31 4.863 0 8 0s5.5 2.31 5.5 5.25c0 1.516-.701 2.5-1.328 3.259-.095.115-.184.22-.268.319-.207.245-.383.453-.541.681-.208.3-.33.565-.37.847a.751.751 0 0 1-1.485-.212c.084-.593.337-1.078.621-1.489.203-.292.45-.584.673-.848.075-.088.147-.173.213-.253.561-.679.985-1.32.985-2.304 0-2.06-1.637-3.75-4-3.75ZM5.75 12h4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-4.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5ZM6 15.25a.75.75 0 0 1 .75-.75h2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5h-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-.75-.75Z"></path></svg>Tip</p><p dir="auto">If you have not installed Docker on your local machine (Windows, Mac, or Linux), see <a href="https://docs.docker.com/engine/install/" rel="nofollow">Install Docker Engine</a>.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Start up the server</h3><a id="user-content--start-up-the-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Start up the server" href="#-start-up-the-server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Ensure <code>vm.max_map_count</code> &gt;= 262144:</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">To check the value of <code>vm.max_map_count</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sysctl vm.max_map_count"><pre>sysctl vm.max_map_count</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reset <code>vm.max_map_count</code> to a value at least 262144 if it is not.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# In this case, we set it to 262144:
sudo sysctl -w vm.max_map_count=262144"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> In this case, we set it to 262144:</span>
sudo sysctl -w vm.max_map_count=262144</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This change will be reset after a system reboot. To ensure your change remains permanent, add or update the
<code>vm.max_map_count</code> value in <strong>/etc/sysctl.conf</strong> accordingly:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="vm.max_map_count=262144"><pre>vm.max_map_count=262144</pre></div>
</blockquote>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Clone the repo:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git"><pre>git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Start up the server using the pre-built Docker images:</p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-caution" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-stop mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4.47.22A.749.749 0 0 1 5 0h6c.199 0 .389.079.53.22l4.25 4.25c.141.14.22.331.22.53v6a.749.749 0 0 1-.22.53l-4.25 4.25A.749.749 0 0 1 11 16H5a.749.749 0 0 1-.53-.22L.22 11.53A.749.749 0 0 1 0 11V5c0-.199.079-.389.22-.53Zm.84 1.28L1.5 5.31v5.38l3.81 3.81h5.38l3.81-3.81V5.31L10.69 1.5ZM8 4a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v3.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-3.5A.75.75 0 0 1 8 4Zm0 8a1 1 0 1 1 0-2 1 1 0 0 1 0 2Z"></path></svg>Caution</p><p dir="auto">All Docker images are built for x86 platforms. We don't currently offer Docker images for ARM64.
If you are on an ARM64 platform, follow <a href="https://ragflow.io/docs/dev/build_docker_image" rel="nofollow">this guide</a> to build a Docker image compatible with your system.</p>
</div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">The command below downloads the <code>v0.27.1</code> edition of the RAGFlow Docker image. See the following table for descriptions of different RAGFlow editions. To download a RAGFlow edition different from <code>v0.27.1</code>, update the <code>RAGFLOW_IMAGE</code> variable accordingly in <strong>docker/.env</strong> before using <code>docker compose</code> to start the server.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="   cd ragflow/docker

   git checkout v0.27.1
   # Optional: use a stable tag (see releases: https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow/releases)
   # This step ensures the **entrypoint.sh** file in the code matches the Docker image version.

   # Use CPU for DeepDoc tasks:
   docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d

   # To use GPU to accelerate DeepDoc tasks:
   # sed -i '1i DEVICE=gpu' .env
   # docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d"><pre>   <span class="pl-c1">cd</span> ragflow/docker

   git checkout v0.27.1
   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Optional: use a stable tag (see releases: https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow/releases)</span>
   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> This step ensures the **entrypoint.sh** file in the code matches the Docker image version.</span>

   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use CPU for DeepDoc tasks:</span>
   docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d

   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> To use GPU to accelerate DeepDoc tasks:</span>
   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> sed -i '1i DEVICE=gpu' .env</span>
   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Note: Prior to <code>v0.22.0</code>, we provided both images with embedding models and slim images without embedding models. Details as follows:</p>
</blockquote>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>

</tr>

</tr>

</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Starting with <code>v0.22.0</code>, we ship only the slim edition and no longer append the <strong>-slim</strong> suffix to the image tag.</p>
</blockquote>
<ol start="4" dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Check the server status after having the server up and running:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker logs -f docker-ragflow-cpu-1"><pre>docker logs -f docker-ragflow-cpu-1</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>The following output confirms a successful launch of the system:</em></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="

     / __ <span class="pl-cce">\ </span>/   <span class="pl-k">|</span>  / ____// ____// /____  _      __
    / /_/ // /<span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> / / __ / /_   / // __ <span class="pl-cce">\|</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span> /<span class="pl-k">|</span> / /
   / _, _// ___ <span class="pl-k">|</span>/ /_/ // __/  / // /_/ /<span class="pl-k">|</span> <span class="pl-k">|</span>/ <span class="pl-k">|</span>/ /

<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">If you skip this confirmation step and directly log in to RAGFlow, your browser may prompt a <code>network abnormal</code>
error because, at that moment, your RAGFlow may not be fully initialized.</p>
</blockquote>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">In your web browser, enter the IP address of your server and log in to RAGFlow.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">With the default settings, you only need to enter <code>http://IP_OF_YOUR_MACHINE</code> (<strong>sans</strong> port number) as the default
HTTP serving port <code>80</code> can be omitted when using the default configurations.</p>
</blockquote>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">In <a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/service_conf.yaml.template">service_conf.yaml.template</a>, select the desired LLM factory in <code>user_default_llm</code> and update
the <code>API_KEY</code> field with the corresponding API key.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="https://ragflow.io/docs/dev/llm_api_key_setup" rel="nofollow">llm_api_key_setup</a> for more information.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><em>The show is on!</em></p>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔧 Configurations</h2><a id="user-content--configurations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔧 Configurations" href="#-configurations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When it comes to system configurations, you will need to manage the following files:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/.env">.env</a>: Keeps the fundamental setups for the system, such as <code>SVR_HTTP_PORT</code>, <code>MYSQL_PASSWORD</code>, and
<code>MINIO_PASSWORD</code>.</li>
<li><a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/service_conf.yaml.template">service_conf.yaml.template</a>: Configures the back-end services. The environment variables in this file will be automatically populated when the Docker container starts. Any environment variables set within the Docker container will be available for use, allowing you to customize service behavior based on the deployment environment.</li>
<li><a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/docker-compose.yml">docker-compose.yml</a>: The system relies on <a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/docker-compose.yml">docker-compose.yml</a> to start up.</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/README.md">./docker/README</a> file provides a detailed description of the environment settings and service
configurations which can be used as <code>${ENV_VARS}</code> in the <a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/service_conf.yaml.template">service_conf.yaml.template</a> file.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">To update the default HTTP serving port (80), go to <a href="/infiniflow/ragflow/blob/main/docker/docker-compose.yml">docker-compose.yml</a> and change <code>80:80</code>
to <code>&lt;YOUR_SERVING_PORT&gt;:80</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Updates to the above configurations require a reboot of all containers to take effect:</p>
<blockquote>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d"><pre>docker compose -f docker-compose.yml up -d</pre></div>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Switch doc engine from Elasticsearch to Infinity</h3><a id="user-content-switch-doc-engine-from-elasticsearch-to-infinity" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Switch doc engine from Elasticsearch to Infinity" href="#switch-doc-engine-from-elasticsearch-to-infinity"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">RAGFlow uses Elasticsearch by default for storing full text and vectors. To switch to <a href="https://github.com/infiniflow/infinity/">Infinity</a>, follow these steps:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Stop all running containers:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml down -v"><pre>docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml down -v</pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-warning" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-alert mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p><p dir="auto"><code>-v</code> will delete the docker container volumes, and the existing data will be cleared.</p>
</div>
<ol start="2" dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Set <code>DOC_ENGINE</code> in <strong>docker/.env</strong> to <code>infinity</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Start the containers:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml up -d"><pre>docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml up -d</pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-warning" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-alert mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p><p dir="auto">Switching to Infinity on a Linux/arm64 machine is not yet officially supported.</p>
</div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔧 Build a Docker Image</h2><a id="user-content--build-a-docker-image" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔧 Build a Docker Image" href="#-build-a-docker-image"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This image is approximately 2 GB in size and relies on external LLM and embedding services.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git
cd ragflow/
docker build --platform linux/amd64 -f Dockerfile -t infiniflow/ragflow:nightly ."><pre>git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> ragflow/
docker build --platform linux/amd64 -f Dockerfile -t infiniflow/ragflow:nightly <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or if you are behind a proxy, you can pass proxy arguments:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker build --platform linux/amd64 \
  --build-arg http_proxy=http://YOUR_PROXY:PORT \
  --build-arg https_proxy=http://YOUR_PROXY:PORT \
  -f Dockerfile -t infiniflow/ragflow:nightly ."><pre>docker build --platform linux/amd64 \
  --build-arg http_proxy=http://YOUR_PROXY:PORT \
  --build-arg https_proxy=http://YOUR_PROXY:PORT \
  -f Dockerfile -t infiniflow/ragflow:nightly <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🔨 Launch Service from Source for Development</h2><a id="user-content--launch-service-from-source-for-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🔨 Launch Service from Source for Development" href="#-launch-service-from-source-for-development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important" dir="auto"><p class="markdown-alert-title" dir="auto"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p dir="auto">After cloning the repository for the first time, run <code>git config --local --unset core.hooksPath</code>, <code>uv tool install lefthook</code> and <code>lefthook install</code> once from the repo root to enable local Git hooks.</p>
</div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Install <code>uv</code>, or skip this step if it is already installed:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pipx install uv"><pre>pipx install uv</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Clone the source code and install Python dependencies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git
cd ragflow/
uv sync --python 3.13 # install RAGFlow dependent python modules
uv run python3 ragflow_deps/download_deps.py
git config --local --unset core.hooksPath
uv tool install lefthook
lefthook install"><pre>git clone https://github.com/infiniflow/ragflow.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> ragflow/
uv sync --python 3.13 <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install RAGFlow dependent python modules</span>
uv run python3 ragflow_deps/download_deps.py
git config --local --unset core.hooksPath
uv tool install lefthook
lefthook install</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Launch the dependent services (MinIO, Elasticsearch, Redis, and MySQL) using Docker Compose:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker compose -f docker/docker-compose-base.yml up -d"><pre>docker compose -f docker/docker-compose-base.yml up -d</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Add the following line to <code>/etc/hosts</code> to resolve all hosts specified in <strong>docker/.env</strong> to <code>127.0.0.1</code>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="127.0.0.1       es01 infinity mysql minio redis sandbox-executor-manager"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>127.0.0.1       es01 infinity mysql minio redis sandbox-executor-manager
</code></pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">If you cannot access HuggingFace, set the <code>HF_ENDPOINT</code> environment variable to use a mirror site:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com"><pre><span class="pl-k">export</span> HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">If your operating system does not have jemalloc, please install it as follows:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Ubuntu
sudo apt-get install libjemalloc-dev
# CentOS
sudo yum install jemalloc
# OpenSUSE
sudo zypper install jemalloc
# macOS
brew install jemalloc"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Ubuntu</span>
sudo apt-get install libjemalloc-dev
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> CentOS</span>
sudo yum install jemalloc
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> OpenSUSE</span>
sudo zypper install jemalloc
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> macOS</span>
brew install jemalloc</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Launch backend service:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="source .venv/bin/activate
export PYTHONPATH=$(pwd)
bash docker/launch_backend_service.sh"><pre><span class="pl-c1">source</span> .venv/bin/activate
<span class="pl-k">export</span> PYTHONPATH=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>pwd<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span>
bash docker/launch_backend_service.sh</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Install frontend dependencies:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cd web
npm install"><pre><span class="pl-c1">cd</span> web
npm install</pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Launch frontend service:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm run dev"><pre>npm run dev</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><em>The following output confirms a successful launch of the system:</em></p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/93570324/371018995-0daf462c-a24d-4496-a66f-92533534e187.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODg1MDgyNDAsIm5iZiI6MTc4ODUwNzk0MCwicGF0aCI6Ii85MzU3MDMyNC8zNzEwMTg5OTUtMGRhZjQ2MmMtYTI0ZC00NDk2LWE2NmYtOTI1MzM1MzRlMTg3LnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA5MDQlMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwOTA0VDA3NDU0MFomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTVkZjZmN2YzYWU4YTM2ZDllMDAxMjUyNDkzYzE5M2MwMmRkY2M5ZGQwOGUwNDgzODM0ZGEzOGUwZDVkMDY0ODcmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.WXFSMMyP79dXDBgvan_sPb4O2s0bFB4NXkCBujjajMw"><img src="https://private-user-images.githubusercontent.com/93570324/371018995-0daf462c-a24d-4496-a66f-92533534e187.png?jwt=eyJ0eXAiOiJKV1QiLCJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJnaXRodWIuY29tIiwiYXVkIjoicmF3LmdpdGh1YnVzZXJjb250ZW50LmNvbSIsImtleSI6ImtleTUiLCJleHAiOjE3ODg1MDgyNDAsIm5iZiI6MTc4ODUwNzk0MCwicGF0aCI6Ii85MzU3MDMyNC8zNzEwMTg5OTUtMGRhZjQ2MmMtYTI0ZC00NDk2LWE2NmYtOTI1MzM1MzRlMTg3LnBuZz9YLUFtei1BbGdvcml0aG09QVdTNC1ITUFDLVNIQTI1NiZYLUFtei1DcmVkZW50aWFsPUFLSUFWQ09EWUxTQTUzUFFLNFpBJTJGMjAyNjA5MDQlMkZ1cy1lYXN0LTElMkZzMyUyRmF3czRfcmVxdWVzdCZYLUFtei1EYXRlPTIwMjYwOTA0VDA3NDU0MFomWC1BbXotRXhwaXJlcz0zMDAmWC1BbXotU2lnbmF0dXJlPTVkZjZmN2YzYWU4YTM2ZDllMDAxMjUyNDkzYzE5M2MwMmRkY2M5ZGQwOGUwNDgzODM0ZGEzOGUwZDVkMDY0ODcmWC1BbXotU2lnbmVkSGVhZGVycz1ob3N0JnJlc3BvbnNlLWNvbnRlbnQtdHlwZT1pbWFnZSUyRnBuZyJ9.WXFSMMyP79dXDBgvan_sPb4O2s0bFB4NXkCBujjajMw" alt="RAGFlow web interface" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Stop RAGFlow front-end and back-end service after development is complete:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pkill -f &quot;ragflow_server.py|task_executor.py&quot;"><pre>pkill -f <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ragflow_server.py|task_executor.py<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
</li>
</ol>
